## Struct fields that do not cross the bridge

- `crypto/x509.Certificate.PublicKey`: type any
- `crypto/x509/pkix.AttributeTypeAndValue.Value`: type any
- `github.com/ipfs/boxo/namesys.PublishOptions.IPNSOptions`: functional options: they cross only as call arguments
- `github.com/ipfs/go-datastore.Feature.Interface`: type any
- `github.com/ipfs/go-datastore.Feature.DatastoreInterface`: type any
- `github.com/ipfs/go-ipfs-cmds.Command.PreRun`: func(req *github.com/ipfs/go-ipfs-cmds.Request, env github.com/ipfs/go-ipfs-cmds.Environment) error
- `github.com/ipfs/go-ipfs-cmds.Command.PostRun`: tipo github.com/ipfs/go-ipfs-cmds.PostRunMap
- `github.com/ipfs/go-ipfs-cmds.Command.Encoders`: tipo github.com/ipfs/go-ipfs-cmds.EncoderMap
- `github.com/ipfs/go-ipfs-cmds.Command.Type`: type any
- `github.com/ipfs/go-ipfs-cmds.Command.Subcommands`: map[string]

## Adaptations (generator rules and hand-written code)

- One import, `package:p2p_ffi/p2p_ffi.dart`. A package's functions, constants and variables are reached through the namespace tree: the top-level constant `go`, then one getter per segment of the package's path after the host of its URL, the author first, lowerCamelCase as Dart names constants and getters (`github.com/ipfs/boxo/mfs` → `go.ipfs.boxo.mfs.newRoot(...)`, `github.com/ipfs/go-cid` → `go.ipfs.cid`, `github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p-kad-dht` → `go.libp2p.libp2pKadDht`, `golang.org/x/crypto` → `go.x.crypto`, `context` → `go.context`); the module's own segment drops `go-` and its major version goes after it, a nested module keeps the outer one's path, a host of its own has no author (`lukechampine.com/blake3` → `go.blake3`), and the Go standard library sits beside the authors. Dart has no nested types, so a type is a top-level class named after its package's path without the author, minus the words the Go name already starts with (`mfs.Root` → `BoxoMfsRoot`, `peer.ID` → `Libp2pCorePeerId`, `cid.Cid` → `Cid`, `context.Context` → `Context`); when two types get the same class, the one with the longer Go name keeps it whole after a `_` (owner's decision, 2026-10-29): after its path (`grpc.BackoffConfig` → `Grpc_BackoffConfig`, beside `grpc/backoff.Config` → `GrpcBackoffConfig`), or at its own `_` (`errorspb.EncodedError_Leaf` → `ErrorsErrorspbEncodedError_Leaf`), declared under `// ignore: camel_case_types`. Every name is unique in the import. The code of each package is one library under `lib/go/` (`lib/go/ipfs/boxo/mfs.dart`), which adds the members to its namespace class as an extension (`GoIpfsBoxoMfsApi on GoIpfsBoxoMfs`); `lib/src/go.dart` holds the tree and exports them all, and the generator refuses two equal names, a member that hides a sub-namespace, and an `export` in a public library outside `lib/p2p_ffi.dart`.
- A package constant is a getter of one of two layers of its namespace (owner's decision, 2026-10-29), so it does not hide a sub-package (`crypto.MD5` and `crypto/md5`): `enums`, a constant of a named type (`go.multiformats.multicodec.enums.raw`, `go.crypto.enums.md5`), and `consts`, the others (`go.ipfs.cid.consts.raw`); not a Dart `const`. Likewise a function or variable of a type named `...Option` (a functional option, or grpc's `CallOption`, an interface) is a member of the layer `options` (`grpc.Peer` and `grpc/peer`: `go.grpc.options.peer(p)`, `go.libp2p.libp2p.options.listenAddrStrings(...)`); any other member with the name of a sub-package gets a `$` (`go.cockroachdb.errors.join$`; owner's decisions, 2026-10-30); a variable that holds option constructors stays a member of the namespace (`go.ipfs.kubo.core.coreiface.options.unixfs.pin(true, '')`). Its value is written into the Dart code, except when it is not the same on every target (`go/gen/platform.go`: windows, linux, android; `go.os.consts.devNull`, `go.os.consts.pathSeparator`): then the getter asks the host, which has the value of the platform that runs.
- `context` (`go.context`, hand-written in `lib/go/context.dart`): `background`, `withCancel`, `withTimeout`, `withDeadline`; `ContextCancelFunc` is callable (`cancel()`).
- `readerStream` (exported by `package:p2p_ffi/p2p_ffi.dart`, hand-written): any `io.Reader` handle as a `Stream<Uint8List>`, in 256 KiB pieces; cancel through the reader's context.
- `config.Profiles` (hand-written): `go.ipfs.kubo.config.profiles[name]!.transform(cfg)`; in Go it is a map of functions.
- `ipfs dag put`/`ipfs dag get` (`go.ipfs.kubo.core.commands.dag.dagPut`/`dagGet`, hand-written in `lib/go/ipfs/kubo/core/commands/dag.dart`, with the generated result types of the other `ipfs dag` commands in its `dag.g.dart` part, hand port in `go/manual_dag.go`): in kubo they are the body of the commands, not a library function; same defaults as the CLI (dag-json → dag-cbor, 2 MiB limit), default hash `sha2-256`.
- `ipfs dag export`/`ipfs dag import` (CAR files; same library as `dagPut`/`dagGet`, hand port in `go/manual_car.go`): `dagExport`/`dagExportFile` (CARv1, `localOnly` = `--local-only`) and `dagImport`/`dagImportFiles` (CARv1/v2, `pinRoots` = `--pin-roots`, returns roots and statistics); they take the `IpfsNode`, like the command. No progress bar nor fast-provide of the whole DAG; the root is announced by the CoreAPI's `Provide`.
- Pubsub: the node needs `node.BuildCfg(extraOpts: {'pubsub': true})` (the daemon's `Pubsub.Enabled`); `sub.next(ctx)` delivers one message at a time (example 08 turns it into a `Stream`).
- A concrete handle that implements an exposed interface extends its class (`merkledag.RawNode`/`ProtoNode` → `Node`), to be accepted where Go accepts the interface; a struct that embeds an interface is a handle, not a DTO.
- A map keyed by a value type (`map[peer.ID][]Multiaddr`) becomes `Map<Id, List<Multiaddr>>`; keyed by an integer (`map[multicodec.Code]uint64`), `Map<int, int>`.
- A struct of exported fields that is an object (`linking.LinkSystem`, `go-datastore.NullDatastore`, table `handleTypes`) is a handle; the handle of a struct returned by value keeps the pointer (it is valid as `T` and `*T`).
- DTO method (`car.Header.hasIndex`, `cid.Prefix.sum`): the receiver goes by value; what the method changes in it does not come back.
- Value type method (`cid.Cid.version`, `peer.Id.extractPublicKey`, `multiaddr.Multiaddr.encapsulate`): the receiver goes as its string, like a value parameter; Go's `String()` is the class's `value`, so it is not listed.
- A getters-only interface (snapshot, `blocks.Block`) as a parameter: Dart builds the class (`Block(cid: c, rawData: d)`) and Go receives a struct that returns those values.
- A `chan<- T` channel passed in an option (`options.Unixfs.Events`) becomes a `StreamSink<T>` (`options.unixfs.events(controller.sink)`); the real item type of a `chan<- any` comes from the generator's `chanElem` table (`AddEvent`). Like `ipfs add`, the channel is closed when the call returns: every event arrives and the sink closes before the `Future` completes.
- A package variable becomes a getter; a named function type (`libp2p.RoutingOption`) is an opaque handle, created only in Go; an unnamed func identical to a named type of the package, or of one it imports, uses the named type (`bsfetcher.DefaultPrototypeChooser`).
- A function parameter that returns nothing, `bool` or `error` (a callback) becomes a Dart function (`idx.forEach((mh, offset) {...})`): Go calls a real callback, which takes the arguments to Dart in batches and waits for the verdict; `false` or an exception stops Go, and the call throws the original Dart exception. `batchSize` (optional): invocations per batch, default 1024 (up to 128 KiB, measured) or 1 when the callback receives Go objects; Go stays at most `batchSize - 1` ahead. The call's objects are in use (`isBusy`, `P2pService.busy`); a call to them from inside the callback that waits on a lock the Go function holds becomes `GolangDeadlockException` (WIRE.md, "Callback").
- A function Go keeps past the call (a handler; table `retainedFuncs`: `host.setStreamHandler`) is a retained callback (WIRE.md): Go calls it, on the Dart event loop, for as long as it holds it, and Dart drops it when Go's garbage collector frees it. A root that is an interface type (`network.Notifiee`, `mdns.Notifee`) becomes a factory with one optional function per method (`Notifee(handlePeerFound: (pi) {...})`), each a retained callback. An exception in a retained callback goes to the zone (like a `Timer`'s).
- A callback of a `pkg.*` root nobody checked (Go may call it during the call or keep it) is a retained callback by default (owner's decision, 2026-10-29): the call is asynchronous, Go waits for each invocation, so all of those made during the call run before its `Future` completes, and the call's objects are in use, as with a callback (`GolangDeadlockException`). Read in Go, it becomes an explicit root in roots.txt (called only during the call: batches, the call throws the Dart exception) or a line of `retainedFuncs` (kept: Go does not wait when it returns nothing).
- Functional options built in another package come with it: libp2p's (`libp2p.options.listenAddrStrings(...)`) build a `config.Option` (`libp2p.Option` is its alias), and a package variable of the option's type is a getter of the same layer (`libp2p.options.noListenAddrs`). The type of an internal package seen through an exported alias of the same name lives in the alias's library (`dht.Option`).
- An `any` parameter that takes one Go type (table `anyAs`: `pubsub.registerTopicValidator`'s `val` is a `ValidatorEx`) crosses as that type; a retained callback may return a basic value (`pubsub.validationAccept`), and `cbOnThrow` says what Go gets when the Dart function throws (`validationReject`).
- Tokens (`go/gen/tokens.go`, WIRE.md tag 9): a listed struct type has `static const $type`, which Go turns into `new(T)` where a parameter takes the type by example (`bus.subscribe(event.EvtPeerConnectednessChanged.$type)`, or a list of them), and its values come out of a dynamic value as the class (`sub.out()`); a listed Go function is a `<name>Func` constant that Go gets as the function itself (`libp2p.options.transport(tcp.newTcpTransportFunc)`).
- An option constructor takes what a function takes: a struct (`pubsub.options.withGossipSubParams(pubsub.defaultGossipSubParams()..d = 8)`), and a function it keeps becomes a retained callback (`pubsub.options.withMessageFilter((m) => ...)`, `dht.options.onRequestHook`); an option inside an option stays one (`namesys.ResolveOption`).
- A trailing variadic whose type does not cross is left out: Go gets none (`event.Bus.Subscribe`'s `opts`), noted under the package.
- `time.Duration` is a Dart `Duration` (nanoseconds on the wire, rounded to microseconds in Dart).
- Errors that wrap `io.EOF`, `io.ErrUnexpectedEOF`, `context.Canceled`, `context.DeadlineExceeded` or IPLD's not found become `GolangEofException`, `GolangUnexpectedEofException`, `GolangCanceledException`, `GolangDeadlineExceededException` and `GolangNotFoundException` (subclasses of `GolangException`), as Go's `errors.Is` would find them. Every other exported error sentinel or error type of a package with a library has a `GolangException` subclass there (section "Go errors with a Dart class"): the host walks the error chain (`Unwrap`, `errors.Join`) and Dart throws the class of the outermost link that has one. Every `GolangException` carries `errTexts` (the text of each link, outside in), `errText` (the innermost), `errno` (the chain's `syscall.Errno`: POSIX numbers on linux and Android, Win32 on Windows) and `causes` (the links with a class); `isA<T>()`/`find<T>()` are `errors.Is`/`errors.As`.
- A root that is only a struct type (`bsadapter.Adapter`, `basicnode.Prototype__Any`) becomes an empty constructor (`Adapter()`, Go's `new(T)`), and its fields, getters and setters.
- An interface that embeds another gets the cast from it (`idx.asCarV2IndexIterableIndex()`, named after the Dart class; Go's `x.(index.IterableIndex)`).
- A handle is accepted wherever Go accepts it: Dart has one base class, so each interface's methods are also a mixin (`Libp2pCoreRoutingValueStore$`), and a type mixes in and implements every exposed interface it implements in Go besides its base class's (`Libp2pKadDhtIpfsDht` is a `Libp2pCoreRoutingContentRouting` and a `Libp2pCoreRoutingValueStore`).
- An element of a list or map is not nullable: a nil one Go sends (a nil pointer, a nil error in a `[]error`) throws `GolangException('Go returned nil')`.
- A Go declaration whose doc says `Deprecated:` is `@Deprecated` with Go's text: it is there, and the analyzer warns.
- A pointer to a basic type (`DagByteRange.To *int64`) becomes `int?`: null is nil.
- A function that returns an unexported type is seen in Dart through an exported interface (table `resultAs`: `merkledag.NewDAGService` → `format.DAGService`); on the wire it is the object's handle.
- A type defined from an interface of another package (`type BaseBlocks blockstore.Blockstore`) gets no methods of its own: list them as roots on the type itself (`node.BaseBlocks.Has`).
- The same Go object always has the same handle, wherever it comes from (a cast, a field read again).
- Methods and functions with a `context.Context` are asynchronous (`Future`); channels become `Stream`; `(T, error)` becomes `T` with `GolangException`.
- Variadics become an optional `List`; functional options mirror the package variables (`options.unixfs.pin(true, '')`).
- A synchronous `NewX` that returns the handle `X` of the same package becomes `factory X(...)` (not for a DTO: `car.newHeader` is a function).
- An `io.Writer` parameter is a `GoHandle?`: a Go object that implements it (`os.File`, `bytes.Buffer`); null = `io.Discard`.
- `fsrepo.Open`: the host cleans the path (`repoPath`, the form `Repo.Path()` returns) before opening, so the config commands, which reopen the repo by `Path()`, reuse the open repo (`fsrepo` keys the open one by the raw string).
- `uint64` integers travel as the same 64 bits in a Dart `int` (like `Uint64List`): from 2^63 up they read as negative; `BigInt.from(v).toUnsigned(64)` reads them unsigned (each field's doc warns).
- An I/O interface parameter (`io.Reader`, `io.ReaderAt`, `io.ReadSeeker`, `car.SectionReader`...) accepts a `Uint8List` (when a `bytes.Reader` implements it) or a handle that implements it (kubo's `files.File`, `os.open`).
- Value types (CID, path, peer ID, multiaddr) keep the string; validation happens in Go, when they cross.
- `config.Config` keeps kubo's JSON (the same as the repo's `config` file) in `json`.
- A Go `nil` result where Dart expects an object becomes `GolangException`.
- Kubo runs in `p2p_host`, in another process (`P2pService.start()` before the API; protocol in `WIRE.md`): a Go panic inside a call is recovered and thrown as `GolangPanicException` (with the Go stack; close and remake the object the call touched), and one in a goroutine of the library stops only the service, whose pending calls fail with `P2pServiceException`.
- Handles are freed by the Dart `Finalizer` (in batches, through the service's Free function) and are only valid in the service they were born in; Go resources (node, repo) still need Go's own `Close`.

## Go errors with a Dart class

Each is a `GolangException` subclass in the library of its package: thrown when it is
the outermost link of the error chain with a class, otherwise in `causes`
(`isA`, `find`). Fields that are another `error` are the next link
of the chain.

| Go | Dart | Fields |
|---|---|---|
| `var bytes.ErrTooLarge error` | `BytesErrTooLarge` in `lib/go/bytes.dart` |  |
| `x509.CertificateInvalidError` | `CryptoX509CertificateInvalidError` in `lib/go/crypto/x509.dart` | reason, detail (not crossing: Cert) |
| `x509.ConstraintViolationError` | `CryptoX509ConstraintViolationError` in `lib/go/crypto/x509.dart` |  |
| `var x509.ErrUnsupportedAlgorithm error` | `CryptoX509ErrUnsupportedAlgorithm` in `lib/go/crypto/x509.dart` |  |
| `x509.HostnameError` | `CryptoX509HostnameError` in `lib/go/crypto/x509.dart` | host (not crossing: Certificate) |
| `var x509.IncorrectPasswordError error` | `CryptoX509IncorrectPasswordError` in `lib/go/crypto/x509.dart` |  |
| `x509.InsecureAlgorithmError` | `CryptoX509InsecureAlgorithmError` in `lib/go/crypto/x509.dart` |  |
| `x509.SystemRootsError` | `CryptoX509SystemRootsError` in `lib/go/crypto/x509.dart` | (not crossing: Err (the next link of the chain)) |
| `x509.UnhandledCriticalExtension` | `CryptoX509UnhandledCriticalExtension` in `lib/go/crypto/x509.dart` |  |
| `x509.UnknownAuthorityError` | `CryptoX509UnknownAuthorityError` in `lib/go/crypto/x509.dart` | (not crossing: Cert) |
| `secp256k1.Error` | `Secp256k1V4Error` in `lib/go/decred/dcrd/dcrec/secp256k1/v4.dart` | description (not crossing: Err (the next link of the chain)) |
| `secp256k1.ErrorKind` | `Secp256k1V4ErrorKind` in `lib/go/decred/dcrd/dcrec/secp256k1/v4.dart` |  |
| `dig.PanicError` | `DigPanicError` in `lib/go/dig.dart` | (not crossing: Panic) |
| `var io.ErrClosedPipe error` | `IoErrClosedPipe` in `lib/go/io.dart` |  |
| `var io.ErrNoProgress error` | `IoErrNoProgress` in `lib/go/io.dart` |  |
| `var io.ErrShortBuffer error` | `IoErrShortBuffer` in `lib/go/io.dart` |  |
| `var io.ErrShortWrite error` | `IoErrShortWrite` in `lib/go/io.dart` |  |
| `var fs.ErrClosed error` | `IoFsErrClosed` in `lib/go/io/fs.dart` |  |
| `var fs.ErrExist error` | `IoFsErrExist` in `lib/go/io/fs.dart` |  |
| `var fs.ErrInvalid error` | `IoFsErrInvalid` in `lib/go/io/fs.dart` |  |
| `var fs.ErrNotExist error` | `IoFsErrNotExist` in `lib/go/io/fs.dart` |  |
| `var fs.ErrPermission error` | `IoFsErrPermission` in `lib/go/io/fs.dart` |  |
| `fs.PathError` | `IoFsPathError` in `lib/go/io/fs.dart` | op, path (not crossing: Err (the next link of the chain)) |
| `var fs.SkipAll error` | `IoFsSkipAll` in `lib/go/io/fs.dart` |  |
| `var fs.SkipDir error` | `IoFsSkipDir` in `lib/go/io/fs.dart` |  |

texts):

- `type storage.ErrNotFound struct{Cid cid.Cid}`: has its runtime class (`GolangEofException`, `GolangNotFoundException`...)


## RPC commands (`ipfs ...`) → `go.ipfs.kubo.core.commands` (`lib/go/ipfs/kubo/core/commands.dart`)

Generated from the kubo command tree (`core/commands.Root`); the host calls them like the kubo HTTP server (function 36 of `WIRE.md`). Each result type lives in the library of its Go package.

Left out:

- `ipfs commands`: the CLI help
- `ipfs commands completion bash`: the CLI help
- `ipfs commands completion fish`: the CLI help
- `ipfs commands completion zsh`: the CLI help
- `ipfs config edit`: opens $EDITOR in the host process
- `ipfs dag export`: hand port with the same name (`go/ipfs/kubo/core/commands/dag.dart`)
- `ipfs dag get`: hand port with the same name (`go/ipfs/kubo/core/commands/dag.dart`)
- `ipfs dag import`: hand port with the same name (`go/ipfs/kubo/core/commands/dag.dart`)
- `ipfs dag put`: hand port with the same name (`go/ipfs/kubo/core/commands/dag.dart`)
- `ipfs mount`: FUSE: mounts on the host's file system
- `ipfs shutdown`: stops the daemon; in p2p_ffi it is `IpfsNode.close`/`P2pService.stop`
- `ipfs update check`: updates the kubo binary (not the case of p2p_host)
- `ipfs update clean`: updates the kubo binary (not the case of p2p_host)
- `ipfs update install`: updates the kubo binary (not the case of p2p_host)
- `ipfs update revert`: updates the kubo binary (not the case of p2p_host)
- `ipfs update versions`: updates the kubo binary (not the case of p2p_host)
